# The Smoke Control Areas (Exempted Fireplaces) (Wales) Order 2014

Made: 12 March 2014

Laid before the National Assembly for Wales: 18 March 2014

Coming into force: 11 April 2014

. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

#### Title, commencement and application

###### 1

. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

#### Fireplaces exempted from section 20 of the Clean Air Act 1993

###### 2

. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

#### Revocation

###### 3

. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .
